最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

F3 · 语言选择存在会话上，同账号多标签页会互相覆盖服务端语言

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-i18n · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

README 明确写出会话单例与同账号多标签页的已知覆盖限制（E4），useAppLocale 的 changeLocale 先写 localStorage 再 PUT 会话（E3），服务端按会话 locale 解析请求（E1）。应用侧使用 useAppLocale/useSyncServerLocale 并按 reason/status 映射自有文案（E45、E46、E41），因此这是框架边界而非应用误用；没有外部环境或工厂覆盖可解释该限制。现有能力只提供稳定 reason 与按请求语言的 localizedMessage，未提供按标签页维度，改进建议成立。

  • 职责与公开约定：包 README 的接口约定明确：语言存于会话，同一账号的标签页会互相覆盖，标签 A 切中文后标签 B 的请求也返回中文而界面仍是英文；PUT 是会话单例替换。候选引用的前提与冻结文档原文一致。 E4
  • 实际行为证据：changeLocale 在加载并切换界面后 writeStoredLocale，再调用 notifyServerLocale 以 PUT 写入会话；useSyncServerLocale 启动时补同步；服务端中间件按会话 locale 解析请求语言。这正好产生候选描述的覆盖链。 E3 E1
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用语言切换器直接使用 useAppLocale，布局在启动时调用 useSyncServerLocale；错误文案按 ApiClientError 的 reason/status 映射到本地翻译键，而不是显示服务端 localizedMessage。未发现应用错误装配或误用 API。 E45 E46 E41
  • 环境因素：覆盖依赖浏览器的会话 cookie 与 localStorage，不需要外部服务或凭据；README 也说明不同浏览器、过期会话、另一标签页切换都会造成漂移。没有缺失的关键环境材料可把现象改归因于环境。 E4 E3
  • 工厂因素：候选 owner 为 plugin；限制位于 @nocobase/app-plugin-i18n 的会话实现和 README，未出现工厂提示、注入配置、QA/构建脚本或模板改写该行为的证据。工厂不是该现象的来源。 E3 E4
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：现有能力是服务端按请求语言生成 localizedMessage 并提供稳定 reason，前端可据 reason 自行翻译；应用已经这样做，所以界面文案不受覆盖影响。但公开入口没有按标签页/客户端维度保存语言的参数，改进空间仍然存在。 E2 E41

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

useAppLocale() 切换后把语言 PUT 到会话（LOCALE_SESSION_KEY='locale'），服务端随后按该值解析请求语言；README 的“已知限制”承认同一账号的多个标签页会互相覆盖：标签 A 切到中文，标签 B 的接口返回也变中文，而它的界面仍是英文。界面语言本身写在 localStorage，同样是浏览器维度共享。

实际影响
本次需求只要求“两位同事的独立会话互不影响”，用不同账号登录即可满足（B06 通过）；但同一人开两个标签页（常见用法）时，服务端返回文案与界面语言会不一致。
建议改法
在语言切换端点上接受可选的语言维度（例如按标签页/客户端 id 存储），或把该边界提升为选型说明，并统一推荐“前端始终按 reason 映射文案、服务端 localizedMessage 仅作 API 消费者兜底”。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
同一账号在两个标签页分别使用中文和英文界面，任一标签页切换语言后发起请求。
应有能力 / 行为
每个界面按自己显示的语言获得文案，或文档明确说明界面语言与服务端应答语言共享同一存储。
实际观察
语言选择写入会话（单一 locale），最后切换者覆盖；另一标签页的接口应答语言随之改变，界面语言仍由 i18next 本地渲染。
应用绕行与代价
本次应用让前端按 reason + 自身界面语言映射文案，不使用服务端 localizedMessage，因此界面文案不受影响；该做法也是 i18n 指引推荐的前端路径。
改进后的验收标准
同一账号两标签页分别设为 zh-CN/en-US，各自触发一次失败请求，两页显示各自语言的提示；或 README/Skill 明确写出“同一账号共享服务端语言”的边界。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 2 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:19–55E3

useAppLocale 的 changeLocale 先 setLocale 加载并切换界面，再 writeStoredLocale 写 localStorage，最后 notifyServerLocale 以 PUT 通知服务端；useSyncServerLocale 在启动时补同步。

/**
 * The current language and a way to change it.
 *
 * Loads and switches the interface before persisting the choice and notifying the server. A server fallback is a
 * successful result the control can explain; transport failures reject without undoing the browser's language.
 */
export function useAppLocale() {
    const api = useApiClient();
    const runtime = useOptionalI18nRuntime();
    const { locale, locales, setLocale, switching, error } = useRuntimeLocale();
    const [syncing, setSyncing] = useState(false);
    const [syncError, setSyncError] = useState();
    const changeLocale = useCallback(async (next) => {
        setSyncing(true);
        setSyncError(undefined);
        try {
            await setLocale(next);
            const selected = runtime?.getLocale() ?? next;
            writeStoredLocale(selected);
            return await notifyServerLocale(api, selected);
        }
        catch (cause) {
            setSyncError(cause instanceof Error ? cause : new Error(String(cause)));
            throw cause;
        }
        finally {
            setSyncing(false);
        }
    }, [api, runtime, setLocale]);
    return {
        locale,
        locales,
        setLocale: changeLocale,
        switching: switching || syncing,
        error: syncError ?? error,
    };
}
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:75–92E4

README 描述切换的四步链、服务端 fallback 语义，并明确“语言存在会话上，同账号多标签页会互相覆盖”的已知限制，以及语言端点与返回值。

## Keeping the server in step

The browser is the source of truth for what it renders; the server keeps its own copy so it can answer in the right language. They drift apart routinely — a different browser, an expired session, a switch made in another tab.

`useSyncServerLocale()` sends the browser's language once at startup to reconcile them. It does not block rendering.

## Endpoints

```
GET /api/i18n/locales   → { data: { defaultLocale, locales: [{ locale, label, direction }] } }
PUT /api/i18n/locale    { locale } → { data: { locale, requestedLocale, fallback } }
```

Both sit under the application's base path, so an application served from `/main` answers at `/main/api/i18n/locale`. The session's language is a singleton setting, so it is replaced with `PUT`. It stores the requested language when supported and otherwise stores English (`en-US`), even if the configured default is another language; that fallback is a successful answer with `fallback: true`, not an error. A missing, empty or non-string `locale`, or any other body field, is answered `400` with reason `INVALID_INPUT` and a field violation in the standard error body.

`GET /api/i18n/locales` is listed in the application's API document at `/api/swagger/docs` under the `I18n` tag (`i18nListLocales`). `PUT /api/i18n/locale` is hidden from it: it stores a preference on the browser's cookie session for the application shell, and a caller with an API key has no session for it to change. What the server offers follows from the application's `server/locales/`; keeping it aligned with `client/locales/` is recommended, but a client-only language is allowed and does not prevent switching.

**A known limit:** the language is stored on the session, so tabs sharing an account overwrite each other. Tab A switching to Chinese means tab B's requests also come back in Chinese while its interface is still English. Error responses carry a stable `reason` alongside the translated `localizedMessage`, so a frontend can render errors in its own interface language from the reason regardless of what the session says.

### [轻微][易用性改进] 语言选择存在会话上，同账号多标签页会互相覆盖服务端语言

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.14）

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

README 明确写出会话单例与同账号多标签页的已知覆盖限制（E4），useAppLocale 的 changeLocale 先写 localStorage 再 PUT 会话（E3），服务端按会话 locale 解析请求（E1）。应用侧使用 useAppLocale/useSyncServerLocale 并按 reason/status 映射自有文案（E45、E46、E41），因此这是框架边界而非应用误用；没有外部环境或工厂覆盖可解释该限制。现有能力只提供稳定 reason 与按请求语言的 localizedMessage，未提供按标签页维度，改进建议成立。

- 职责与公开约定：包 README 的接口约定明确：语言存于会话，同一账号的标签页会互相覆盖，标签 A 切中文后标签 B 的请求也返回中文而界面仍是英文；PUT 是会话单例替换。候选引用的前提与冻结文档原文一致。；证据：E4（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:75–92）
- 实际行为证据：changeLocale 在加载并切换界面后 writeStoredLocale，再调用 notifyServerLocale 以 PUT 写入会话；useSyncServerLocale 启动时补同步；服务端中间件按会话 locale 解析请求语言。这正好产生候选描述的覆盖链。；证据：E3（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:19–55）、E1（packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/server/middleware.js:23–36）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用语言切换器直接使用 useAppLocale，布局在启动时调用 useSyncServerLocale；错误文案按 ApiClientError 的 reason/status 映射到本地翻译键，而不是显示服务端 localizedMessage。未发现应用错误装配或误用 API。；证据：E45（app/client/layouts/components/language-switcher.tsx:20–30）、E46（app/client/layouts/app-layout.tsx:32–36）、E41（app/client/pages/equipments/api.ts:113–127）
- 环境因素：覆盖依赖浏览器的会话 cookie 与 localStorage，不需要外部服务或凭据；README 也说明不同浏览器、过期会话、另一标签页切换都会造成漂移。没有缺失的关键环境材料可把现象改归因于环境。；证据：E4（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:75–92）、E3（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:19–55）
- 工厂因素：候选 owner 为 plugin；限制位于 @nocobase/app-plugin-i18n 的会话实现和 README，未出现工厂提示、注入配置、QA/构建脚本或模板改写该行为的证据。工厂不是该现象的来源。；证据：E3（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:19–55）、E4（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:75–92）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：现有能力是服务端按请求语言生成 localizedMessage 并提供稳定 reason，前端可据 reason 自行翻译；应用已经这样做，所以界面文案不受覆盖影响。但公开入口没有按标签页/客户端维度保存语言的参数，改进空间仍然存在。；证据：E2（packages/@nocobase/i18n/README.md:270–298）、E41（app/client/pages/equipments/api.ts:113–127）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

useAppLocale() 切换后把语言 PUT 到会话（LOCALE_SESSION_KEY='locale'），服务端随后按该值解析请求语言；README 的“已知限制”承认同一账号的多个标签页会互相覆盖：标签 A 切到中文，标签 B 的接口返回也变中文，而它的界面仍是英文。界面语言本身写在 localStorage，同样是浏览器维度共享。

**影响**：本次需求只要求“两位同事的独立会话互不影响”，用不同账号登录即可满足（B06 通过）；但同一人开两个标签页（常见用法）时，服务端返回文案与界面语言会不一致。

**建议改动**：在语言切换端点上接受可选的语言维度（例如按标签页/客户端 id 存储），或把该边界提升为选型说明，并统一推荐“前端始终按 reason 映射文案、服务端 localizedMessage 仅作 API 消费者兜底”。

**验收标准**：同一账号两标签页分别设为 zh-CN/en-US，各自触发一次失败请求，两页显示各自语言的提示；或 README/Skill 明确写出“同一账号共享服务端语言”的边界。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:19–55` — useAppLocale 的 changeLocale 先 setLocale 加载并切换界面，再 writeStoredLocale 写 localStorage，最后 notifyServerLocale 以 PUT 通知服务端；useSyncServerLocale 在启动时补同步。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:75–92` — README 描述切换的四步链、服务端 fallback 语义，并明确“语言存在会话上，同账号多标签页会互相覆盖”的已知限制，以及语言端点与返回值。

F4 · 前端示例文件的引用层级容易猜错，浪费一次取文件调用

文档 / Skill / 示例 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/page.md · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

api.md 的链接是 example/list-page.md，相对 api.md 所在目录正好解析到 references/frontend/references/example/list-page.md；该文件在冻结树中存在，标题为 List page（E22、E40）。实现日志中的失败路径是 references/frontend/references/list-page.md，漏掉了 example/ 这一级（E25），并不是文档链接错误。因此“按 api.md 指引仍打不开或容易猜错层级”的结论被反证，文档链接本身可用。

  • 职责与公开约定：api.md 明确把完整列表示例链接为同目录下的 example/list-page.md；相对链接的基准是 api.md 所在目录，因此目标应为 references/frontend/references/example/list-page.md。文档承诺的路径是完整且自洽的。 E22
  • 实际行为证据：原始工具调用实际读取的是 references/frontend/references/list-page.md，缺少链接中明确写出的 example/ 段，因而报 No such file or directory；没有证据显示 Agent 先按 api.md 的链接打开目标。 E25
  • 应用接入与业务边界：E25 显示这是实现阶段读取资料时的路径失败，同回合随后继续核对语言持久化；没有应用代码调用该路径，也没有应用错误装配或 API 误用的证据。该项不是应用缺陷。 E25
  • 环境因素：冻结文件树中目标文件确实存在（E40），而失败命令的路径少了 example/（E25）；因此不是文件缺失、安装问题或运行环境导致，而是命令拼路径错误。 E40 E25

- List every dependency: `[api, projectId, reloadCount]`. `useApiClient()` returns the same object every time, so it does not cause repeated requests.
- When loading through a Repository (see "Remote Repository"), the request cannot take a `signal` and cannot be aborted, but still check `controller.signal.aborted` in the callbacks to discard stale results.
- For the complete list page (search conditions written to the URL, empty and no-results states, the `Spinner` in the toolbar while reloading), see [`table.md`](table.md) and [`example/list-page.md`](example/list-page.md); for loading the latest data before an edit form opens, see [`form.md`](form.md); for the detail drawer, see [`overlay.md`](overlay.md).

反馈证据核对

E23 实际从 dist/src/server 读到了 createAppTest 声明，exit 2 来自同命令末尾对不存在的 app-test.d.ts 的读取，而不是 grep 无匹配；E24 只显示一次 dist/server/index.d.ts 探测失败，随后 E33 改读 dist/src/server/vitest.d.ts，并非改用 README 入口。package.json 确实 exports 到 dist/src（E44），但 README 首屏入口表和 testing.md 已给出 @nocobase/app-testing/server（E42、E43）。因此只能确认一次路径猜测失败，缺少“反复猜路径”和“指引缺失或无效”的归因证据，不能据此证明文档必须修改。

  • 职责与公开约定：包 README 首屏入口表承诺通过说明符 @nocobase/app-testing/server 使用 createAppTest 等夹具，testing.md 也直接给出同一说明符的 import；package.json 的 exports 把该说明符映射到 dist/src/server。公共契约是说明符，而不是 dist 目录布局。 E42 E43 E44
  • 实际行为证据：原始记录显示：E23 已从 dist/src/server 读取声明；E24 的一次 dist/server/index.d.ts 探测失败；E33 随后从 dist/src/server/vitest.d.ts 读取成功。没有记录显示 Agent 在失败后按 README 入口表定位，也没有多次重复同一失败。 E23 E24 E33
  • 应用接入与业务边界：E24 的失败发生在为是否补应用级集成测试而查阅 app-testing API 时；没有应用代码调用该路径，也没有应用装配或业务逻辑因此出错。候选的“仅增加查找轮次”这一影响没有被扩展成应用缺陷。 E24
  • 环境因素：exports 映射本身在包内且指向 dist/src（E44）；失败只是 node_modules 目录探测。没有外部服务、凭据或安装缺失可解释该现象，也没有材料证明环境阻碍了按说明符导入。 E44
  • 工厂因素：未发现工厂提示、注入配置、QA/构建脚本或模板改写该包布局；候选 owner 为 documentation，现有证据只涉及包产物和 skill 文档，工厂来源无法成立也无法进一步核对。 E44 E43
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：README 入口表与 testing.md 已经提供正确的模块说明符，exports 映射也隐藏了 dist/src 布局（E42、E43、E44）；建议中的“按入口表查 API”已经存在。缺少的是 Agent 是否按该入口查找的证据，而不是公开能力本身。 E42 E43 E44

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [信息][易用性改进] 包内产物路径 dist/src/... 与常见 dist/... 直觉不一致，Agent 反复猜路径

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

E23 实际从 dist/src/server 读到了 createAppTest 声明，exit 2 来自同命令末尾对不存在的 app-test.d.ts 的读取，而不是 grep 无匹配；E24 只显示一次 dist/server/index.d.ts 探测失败，随后 E33 改读 dist/src/server/vitest.d.ts，并非改用 README 入口。package.json 确实 exports 到 dist/src（E44），但 README 首屏入口表和 testing.md 已给出 @nocobase/app-testing/server（E42、E43）。因此只能确认一次路径猜测失败，缺少“反复猜路径”和“指引缺失或无效”的归因证据，不能据此证明文档必须修改。

- 职责与公开约定：包 README 首屏入口表承诺通过说明符 @nocobase/app-testing/server 使用 createAppTest 等夹具，testing.md 也直接给出同一说明符的 import；package.json 的 exports 把该说明符映射到 dist/src/server。公共契约是说明符，而不是 dist 目录布局。；证据：E42（packages/@nocobase/app-testing/README.md:5–9）、E43（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:54–63）、E44（packages/@nocobase/app-testing/package.json:10–14）
- 实际行为证据：原始记录显示：E23 已从 dist/src/server 读取声明；E24 的一次 dist/server/index.d.ts 探测失败；E33 随后从 dist/src/server/vitest.d.ts 读取成功。没有记录显示 Agent 在失败后按 README 入口表定位，也没有多次重复同一失败。；证据：E23（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0010.txt:1464–1477）、E24（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0027.txt:1773–1781）、E33（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0027.txt:1890–1898）
- 应用接入与业务边界：E24 的失败发生在为是否补应用级集成测试而查阅 app-testing API 时；没有应用代码调用该路径，也没有应用装配或业务逻辑因此出错。候选的“仅增加查找轮次”这一影响没有被扩展成应用缺陷。；证据：E24（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0027.txt:1773–1781）
- 环境因素：exports 映射本身在包内且指向 dist/src（E44）；失败只是 node_modules 目录探测。没有外部服务、凭据或安装缺失可解释该现象，也没有材料证明环境阻碍了按说明符导入。；证据：E44（packages/@nocobase/app-testing/package.json:10–14）
- 工厂因素：未发现工厂提示、注入配置、QA/构建脚本或模板改写该包布局；候选 owner 为 documentation，现有证据只涉及包产物和 skill 文档，工厂来源无法成立也无法进一步核对。；证据：E44（packages/@nocobase/app-testing/package.json:10–14）、E43（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:54–63）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：README 入口表与 testing.md 已经提供正确的模块说明符，exports 映射也隐藏了 dist/src 布局（E42、E43、E44）；建议中的“按入口表查 API”已经存在。缺少的是 Agent 是否按该入口查找的证据，而不是公开能力本身。；证据：E42（packages/@nocobase/app-testing/README.md:5–9）、E43（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:54–63）、E44（packages/@nocobase/app-testing/package.json:10–14）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

@nocobase/app-testing 的 exports 指向 dist/src/server/index.js，而 Agent 两次尝试 dist/server/index.d.ts 后才改用 README 记录的入口 '@nocobase/app-testing/server'。同类猜测还包括 @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/server/config.ts（实际在 dist 下）。

**影响**：仅增加查找轮次；包入口本身是正确的，按 README/Skill 的模块说明符导入不会有问题。

**建议改动**：在指引里强调“按包说明符与 README 的入口表查 API，不直接 grep dist 目录”，或在包 README 首屏列出 exports 映射。

**验收标准**：README 首屏或 guidance/testing.md 明确入口说明符与对应文件位置，Agent 无需遍历 dist 即可读到类型。

- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0027.txt:1773–1781` — Agent grep createAppTest 后尝试 dist/server/index.d.ts，路径不存在导致退出码 2，随后改用 dist/src/server。

Fixed the template package name leak in the equipment service token; equipment API, migration and page tests pass again.

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · 验收机制

Prevent template-name leaks at the point a service is written

需人工判断

The equipment service hard-coded the template package name inside its service token. tests/logic/template-package-name.test.ts caught it, but only as a late test failure after the feature was otherwise complete.

建议改动

Document in the services reference that an application service token is a local key such as 'app/<name>-service', never the package name, since create-app rewrites the package name only in client/runtime.ts and client/service-provider.ts.

来源：Agent · retro.json

本次双语设备台账场景中，NocoBase3 的国际化链路（客户端语言资源、localStorage 持久化、会话级服务端语言、按 reason 映射错误文案）、HTTP API 错误契约（ApiError/ALREADY_EXISTS/fieldViolations）与数据层唯一约束都按文档正常工作，Agent 基本沿推荐入口实现，QA 复验 B01–B06 全部通过。已确认的主要不足是能力边界而非缺陷：语言选择存在会话上，同一账号的多个标签页会互相覆盖；国际化指引只示范了插件路由侧 AppI18nError→localizedMessage 的适配，应用自建路由需要自己写约 25 行适配器，本次应用因此完全改用客户端 reason 映射。另有两条指引可发现性小问题（示例文件两级目录、包内 dist/src 布局），只增加排查成本。

E7

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

中英文界面切换：导航、表头、表单、按钮、系统状态、空列表说明都随语言变化，且不显示翻译键。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架负责语言注册表、切换时一次性加载所有 namespace、把选择写入 localStorage 并同步到服务端；应用负责提供两套 locale 文案与业务键。
推荐使用方式
i18n 指引说明语言列表就是应用自己的 client/locales 与 server/locales；插件 Skill 说明 setLocale 会并行加载全部 namespace、更新 <html lang>、写 localStorage 并 PUT /api/i18n/locale，且“不要自己写语言选择器，useAppLocale() 已有模板实现”。
Agent 实际使用
Agent 在 client/locales/en-US.ts 与 zh-CN.ts 中新增 navigation.equipments 与 equipment.* 键，沿用模板的语言开关，未自建 i18n 机制；QA B01/B04 在实际页面逐项核对通过。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E3 E4 E6 E7
英文界面漏填必填项、提交重复编号时显示英文说明；切回中文后同一问题显示中文说明，不能暴露内部错误代码。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架负责稳定的错误契约（reason/domain/fieldViolations/localizedMessage）与按请求语言翻译的能力；应用负责把自己的 reason 映射到两种语言的用户文案，或抛出可翻译错误。
推荐使用方式
i18n 指引明确“前端不直接显示 message，而是把 status/reason 映射到自己的文案”；插件 Skill 规则进一步要求“不要基于已翻译文案分支，比较 reason”。服务端可选 AppI18nError→ApiError(localizedMessage) 适配。
Agent 实际使用
Agent 在服务端定义 EQUIPMENT_CODE_TAKEN/fieldViolations，在客户端按 reason 映射中英文文案，与指引一致；未使用服务端 localizedMessage，因此服务端不产出已翻译文本。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E2 E6 E7
同一浏览器刷新后保留语言；一位同事切换语言不应影响另一位正在使用的同事。正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
框架负责持久化与同步语义，并说明其边界；应用只调用受支持的 hook。
推荐使用方式
插件 README 说明：选择写 localStorage 防刷新丢失，同时把语言存到 session 作为服务端应答语言；客户端用 useSyncServerLocale() 在启动时校正两者。
Agent 实际使用
Agent 使用模板语言开关（走 useAppLocale），QA B05（刷新后仍英文、数据不丢）与 B06（两个不同账号会话各自保持语言）通过。

E1 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/server/middleware.js:23–36

resolveRequestLocale 先读会话存储的 locale，其次 Accept-Language，再落到默认语言；会话不可读时 catch 后降级而非报错。

/**
 * Resolves the locale for a request: the session's stored choice, then `Accept-Language`, then the default.
 */
export async function resolveRequestLocale(runtime, context, options = {}) {
    const stored = await readSessionLocale(context, options.sessionKey ?? LOCALE_SESSION_KEY);
    // The locale endpoint explicitly selects English for a client-only language, even when the application's own
    // server locale list omits it. Base English resources are always loaded alongside the configured default.
    if (stored === BASE_LOCALE)
        return BASE_LOCALE;
    if (stored)
        return runtime.resolveLocale(stored);
    const accepted = parseAcceptLanguage(context.req.header('accept-language') ?? undefined);
    return runtime.resolvePreferredLocale(accepted);
}

原文件 SHA-256：bb228c1f54a1adacfced1b877b8f24fe8c1805232ab81420f6727513d0447fda

E2 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/README.md:270–298

文档说明错误响应的 localizedMessage 是已按请求语言翻译的用户文本，客户端应基于 reason 分支，并可用 metadata 的 ns/key/params 重渲染。

  ),
);
```

The response then carries the error's code as `reason`, the translated text as `localizedMessage`, and the translation inputs in `metadata`:

```jsonc
{
  "error": {
    "code": 400,
    "status": "INVALID_ARGUMENT",
    "reason": "SCHEDULE_TARGET_INVALID",
    "domain": "scheduler",
    "message": "SCHEDULE_TARGET_INVALID: @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler:errors.targetInvalid",
    "localizedMessage": {
      "locale": "zh-CN",
      "message": "执行目标配置无效：缺少 name 字段",
    },
    "metadata": {
      "ns": "@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler",
      "key": "errors.targetInvalid",
      "params": { "field": "name" },
    },
    "requestId": "7f1c9a3e-…",
  },
}
```

Clients branch on `reason`, never on either message. `localizedMessage` is enough for an API-only consumer; a frontend can ignore it and re-render from `metadata.ns`, `metadata.key`, and `metadata.params` in whatever language its interface is currently showing.

原文件 SHA-256：a62ce8fa6eb5d6d3d823462480002eb0af5c15f74a8762eeaad89d76cfae8f33

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:19–55

useAppLocale 的 changeLocale 先 setLocale 加载并切换界面，再 writeStoredLocale 写 localStorage，最后 notifyServerLocale 以 PUT 通知服务端；useSyncServerLocale 在启动时补同步。

/**
 * The current language and a way to change it.
 *
 * Loads and switches the interface before persisting the choice and notifying the server. A server fallback is a
 * successful result the control can explain; transport failures reject without undoing the browser's language.
 */
export function useAppLocale() {
    const api = useApiClient();
    const runtime = useOptionalI18nRuntime();
    const { locale, locales, setLocale, switching, error } = useRuntimeLocale();
    const [syncing, setSyncing] = useState(false);
    const [syncError, setSyncError] = useState();
    const changeLocale = useCallback(async (next) => {
        setSyncing(true);
        setSyncError(undefined);
        try {
            await setLocale(next);
            const selected = runtime?.getLocale() ?? next;
            writeStoredLocale(selected);
            return await notifyServerLocale(api, selected);
        }
        catch (cause) {
            setSyncError(cause instanceof Error ? cause : new Error(String(cause)));
            throw cause;
        }
        finally {
            setSyncing(false);
        }
    }, [api, runtime, setLocale]);
    return {
        locale,
        locales,
        setLocale: changeLocale,
        switching: switching || syncing,
        error: syncError ?? error,
    };
}

原文件 SHA-256：4ac6a653f32beb82cf7bc0ecae597598c004f1703994faabb04019635eeee100

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:75–92

README 描述切换的四步链、服务端 fallback 语义，并明确“语言存在会话上，同账号多标签页会互相覆盖”的已知限制，以及语言端点与返回值。

## Keeping the server in step

The browser is the source of truth for what it renders; the server keeps its own copy so it can answer in the right language. They drift apart routinely — a different browser, an expired session, a switch made in another tab.

`useSyncServerLocale()` sends the browser's language once at startup to reconcile them. It does not block rendering.

## Endpoints

```
GET /api/i18n/locales   → { data: { defaultLocale, locales: [{ locale, label, direction }] } }
PUT /api/i18n/locale    { locale } → { data: { locale, requestedLocale, fallback } }
```

Both sit under the application's base path, so an application served from `/main` answers at `/main/api/i18n/locale`. The session's language is a singleton setting, so it is replaced with `PUT`. It stores the requested language when supported and otherwise stores English (`en-US`), even if the configured default is another language; that fallback is a successful answer with `fallback: true`, not an error. A missing, empty or non-string `locale`, or any other body field, is answered `400` with reason `INVALID_INPUT` and a field violation in the standard error body.

`GET /api/i18n/locales` is listed in the application's API document at `/api/swagger/docs` under the `I18n` tag (`i18nListLocales`). `PUT /api/i18n/locale` is hidden from it: it stores a preference on the browser's cookie session for the application shell, and a caller with an API key has no session for it to change. What the server offers follows from the application's `server/locales/`; keeping it aligned with `client/locales/` is recommended, but a client-only language is allowed and does not prevent switching.

**A known limit:** the language is stored on the session, so tabs sharing an account overwrite each other. Tab A switching to Chinese means tab B's requests also come back in Chinese while its interface is still English. Error responses carry a stable `reason` alongside the translated `localizedMessage`, so a frontend can render errors in its own interface language from the reason regardless of what the session says.

原文件 SHA-256：6c3231dfbe6fa746300cc5954b586e0a4785354ee8ac30ed1715fefb22c2ccbc

E5 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:180–212

服务端翻译配方：请求内用 getRequestTranslator，可抛 AppI18nError，由 router onError 转成带 localizedMessage 与 metadata 的 ApiError。

## Translating on the server

Server text lives in the package's `server/locales/`, wired as above, and is translated in the language of whoever it is for.

Inside a request, the i18n middleware has already resolved the language (session choice, then `Accept-Language`, then the default) and loaded it:

```ts
import { getRequestTranslator } from '@nocobase/i18n/server';

const NS = '@acme/app-plugin-orders';

router.get('/orders/:id', async (context) => {
  const t = getRequestTranslator(context, NS);
  return context.json({
    data: { message: t('orders.archived', { id: context.req.param('id') }) },
  });
});
```

For an error the caller should see in its own language, throw an `AppI18nError` instead of translating a message. It carries a stable code, the namespace, the key and the parameters, and is translated only when it is serialized:

```ts
import { AppI18nError } from '@nocobase/i18n/server';

throw new AppI18nError('ORDER_NOT_FOUND', {
  status: 404,
  ns: NS,
  key: 'errors.notFound',
  params: { id },
});
```

Under `/api` every failure is the standard error body, so an `AppI18nError` that reaches the application on its own is rendered only by its status: its namespace, key, parameters and translated text are lost. The router's `onError` translates it into an `ApiError` and hands everything else on unchanged. The error's code becomes `reason`, the translated text `localizedMessage`, and `ns`, `key` and `params` go in `metadata` so the browser can re-render it in the language its interface is showing:

原文件 SHA-256：12afdb44486ee0f5c1d601ea0f1aa933b00b2ebefdf3cfc383e54f909963c9c0

E6 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:307–315

规则段：不要拼接翻译片段、用真实 runtime 测试、loader key 必须是运行时值，并明确“不要基于已翻译文案分支，比较 reason”。

# Rules

- **Never concatenate translated fragments.** Word order differs by language. Use interpolation: `t('greeting', { name })` against `'Hello {{name}}'`.
- **Test with the real runtime, not a mocked `t`.** Render components under `TestI18nProvider` with a runtime from `createTestI18nRuntime` (`@nocobase/i18n/testing`), built from the package's own locale files. It is strict by default, so a key missing from the fallback chain fails the test even when a `defaultValue` would have hidden it. Pass `defaultValue` only where a tree may genuinely render with no runtime mounted.
- **The loader key must be a runtime value.** `locales['en-US']()` written as a literal lets a bundler drop every other language from the build.
- **Outside a request, load the locale first.** In a background job or cron, `await i18n.ensureLocaleLoaded(locale)` before translating. Skipping it does not throw; translations quietly fall back.
- **`getFixedT` takes the namespace first.** `getFixedT(NS, locale)`; the other order binds a locale as a namespace and translates nothing.
- **Do not branch on a translated message.** Compare the error's `reason` (`ApiClientError.reason` in the browser); its `message` and `localizedMessage` change with the language.
- **Outbound content follows its recipient.** Mail and notifications take an explicit locale — the recipient's, not the locale of whoever triggered the work.

原文件 SHA-256：12afdb44486ee0f5c1d601ea0f1aa933b00b2ebefdf3cfc383e54f909963c9c0

E7 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/i18n.md:5–23

服务端文本来源与语言解析顺序、前端不直接显示 message 而把 status/reason 映射到自有文案、语言列表来自应用自身 locale 文件。

## Server-side text

API error messages, notifications and mail need translation too. The server translates them from `server/locales/`: `en-US.ts` states the wording and exports `AppServerResource`, and every other server locale is annotated with that type, so a key missing from one is a compile error. The template's server locales start empty — the server text that exists belongs to plugins — so add keys when the application's own endpoints start producing text. Name them by the same rules as client keys.

- The server resolves a request's language from the session's stored choice, then `Accept-Language`, then `i18n.defaultLocale`.
- Outbound content follows its recipient: mail, notifications and background work translate into the recipient's language, not the language of whoever triggered the work. A background job has no request to inherit a language from — resolve the recipient's locale explicitly and load it before translating. The `nocobase-app-plugin-i18n` Skill shows how server code translates, inside a request with `getRequestTranslator`, as an `AppI18nError` that the router's `onError` turns into an `ApiError` with a `localizedMessage`, and outside one with `getFixedT(namespace, locale)`.
- The frontend does not display an endpoint's `message` as is; it maps the status and the error's `reason` to its own copy (see [calling endpoints](frontend/references/api.md)).

## Keeping the server in step with the browser

`@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n` owns the language selector, persistence, and the server locale endpoints. The application shell calls `useSyncServerLocale()` so server messages use the browser's language when the server supports it; keep that call when changing the shell. If the server does not have that locale, the browser still switches and the server uses English; the language control shows an informational notice about the fallback.

## Which languages the application offers

The application's own locale files are the list. `client/locales/index.ts` decides what the browser offers and `server/locales/index.ts` what the server does, so adding a language means adding its file and loader entry on the side that needs it — there is no configured list to keep in step.

To add a language to the server, add its file annotated with `AppServerResource` (for example `server/locales/ja-JP.ts`) and its loader entry in `server/locales/index.ts`; the client side is described in [frontend copy and translations](frontend/references/i18n.md).

A plugin's locale file supplies translations for those languages, never a new one. A plugin shipping `ja-JP` to an application that does not offer Japanese puts nothing in the picker. Prefer keeping the client and server lists aligned when server-produced text needs the language. A client-only language is also valid: the interface switches to it, `PUT /api/i18n/locale` records English for the server, and the language control explains the fallback with an informational notice.

E9 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:89–120

recognizeApiError 实现：ApiError 原样返回，HTTPException 映射为 HTTP_<status>，RepositoryError（非 INTERNAL）保留其 status/reason 与 metadata，其它再按 status 错误识别。

/**
 * The `ApiError` for an error the framework recognizes, or `undefined` for one it does not:
 *
 * - an `ApiError` as it is;
 * - Hono's `HTTPException`, and any error that carries a 4xx `status` the way Hono's `getResponse()` convention does
 *   (such as `AuthorizationDeniedError`), with their status and message, and a string `reason` and `domain` on such an
 *   error kept too;
 * - a `RepositoryError` whose `status` is not `INTERNAL` (a refused write, a missing record, a version conflict), with
 *   that status, its code as `reason`, domain `app`, and its `path` and `details` as `metadata`. One that is the
 *   server's own fault, such as an invalid Policy, is not recognized.
 */
export function recognizeApiError(error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError)
        return error;
    if (error instanceof HTTPException) {
        return new ApiError({
            status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(error.status),
            reason: `HTTP_${error.status}`,
            domain: appErrorDomain,
            message: error.message || `Request failed with status ${error.status}.`,
            httpStatus: error.status,
            cause: error,
        });
    }
    const repositoryError = repositoryApiError(error);
    if (repositoryError)
        return repositoryError;
    const statusError = readStatusError(error);
    if (statusError) {
        return new ApiError({
            status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(statusError.status),
            reason: statusError.reason ?? `HTTP_${statusError.status}`,

原文件 SHA-256：c515b61600b91235e0749ec39c58ac05f836bbda512c303689cf371363016f3e

E10 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/openapi/describe.d.ts:46–58

/**
 * Validate one part of the request — `param`, `query`, `json`, `header`, `form` or `cookie` — against a Standard Schema
 * such as a zod schema, and declare that schema in the API document. An invalid request is answered `400
 * INVALID_ARGUMENT` with reason `INVALID_INPUT`, domain `app`, and a field violation per issue before the handler
 * runs, exactly as `parseApiInput()` answers; the handler reads the parsed value from `context.req.valid(target)`.
 * Because of that, the document lists the 400 for invalid input on every route with a validator, without the route
 * declaring it.
 *
 * ```ts
 * router.post('/orders/:orderId/cancel', describeRoute({ ... }), apiValidator('param', OrderParams), apiValidator('json', CancelOrderInput), handler);
 * ```
 */
export declare function apiValidator<Schema extends StandardSchemaV1, Target extends keyof ValidationTargets, E extends Env = Env, P extends string = string>(target: Target, schema: Schema, options?: ApiValidatorOptions): MiddlewareHandler<E, P, ApiValidatorInput<Schema, Target>>;

E12 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/http-api.md:103–125

status 语义表（ALREADY_EXISTS=创建已存在、ABORTED=并发冲突等）与 reason/domain/message/localizedMessage/fieldViolations 的用途约定。

- **`status`** is one of a fixed set, and decides the HTTP status. Pick the one that matches:

  | `status`              | HTTP | Use for                                                   |
  | --------------------- | ---- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
  | `INVALID_ARGUMENT`    | 400  | The request itself is malformed or a field is invalid     |
  | `FAILED_PRECONDITION` | 400  | The request is valid, but the resource's state forbids it |
  | `UNAUTHENTICATED`     | 401  | No valid session or API key                               |
  | `PERMISSION_DENIED`   | 403  | Not allowed                                               |
  | `NOT_FOUND`           | 404  | Allowed, but the resource does not exist                  |
  | `ALREADY_EXISTS`      | 409  | Creating something that already exists                    |
  | `ABORTED`             | 409  | A concurrent change won, such as a version conflict       |
  | `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`  | 429  | A rate limit or quota                                     |
  | `INTERNAL`            | 500  | Never thrown on purpose; the application answers it       |
  | `UNAVAILABLE`         | 503  | A dependency is down; retrying later may succeed          |

  Two HTTP statuses exist outside this table, both as an `INVALID_ARGUMENT` that passes `httpStatus`: `413` for a request body over a size limit the route sets or the application's `api.bodyLimit` and `415` for an unsupported request content type. Nothing else overrides the status, and neither covers anything else: a generated output that grows too large is `400 FAILED_PRECONDITION`, and a file with the wrong extension is `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT`. There is no `422`: an invalid request is `INVALID_ARGUMENT` and a valid one the state forbids is `FAILED_PRECONDITION`, both `400`. There is no `502`: a failing upstream is `503 UNAVAILABLE`.

- **`reason`** is what clients branch on: UPPER_SNAKE_CASE, unique within its domain, stable once released. A client never parses `message`.
- **`domain`** is the namespace of whoever defined the reason: the plugin namespace, or the application's name for its own routes. The framework uses `app`. A plugin has one domain even when it serves several URL prefixes: every AI knowledge base error is `aiKnowledgeBases`. An error passed through from another plugin keeps the domain of the plugin that defined its reason, so the users plugin passing on `LAST_ASSIGNMENT` answers with domain `authorization`.
- **`message`** is an English sentence for developers. It is never shown to users.
- **`localizedMessage`**, `{ locale, message }`, is optional user-facing text the handler has already translated with the request's i18n.
- **`fieldViolations`**, `[{ field, description }]`, names the invalid fields of an `INVALID_ARGUMENT`.
- **`metadata`** carries further machine-readable facts about this occurrence.

原文件 SHA-256：9297b5c61d5a78e0d6481405117eca8ac8f9c7eb5dd61a8b0e2bbf5118cdc93c

E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–114

迁移与种子的职责区分，以及含 collection.unique('orderNo') 的完整迁移示例，要求迁移自包含。

## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job. Sample or demonstration content is not install data: declare it as a sample seed, described below, never as a plain seed.

```ts
// database/main/migrations/202609020001_create_orders.ts
import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202609020001_create_orders',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('orders', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('orderNo', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('status', { length: 32, nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.unique('orderNo');
      collection.index('status');
    });
  },

  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('orders');
  },
});

export default migration;
```

LanguageSwitcher 使用 useAppLocale 并提供 setLocale；注释说明插件负责持久化与资源加载，应用未自行改写会话存储。

E46 · app/client/layouts/app-layout.tsx:32–36

  // The browser decides what it renders; this tells the server the same language so its messages match.
  useSyncServerLocale();

E41 · app/client/pages/equipments/api.ts:113–127

equipmentErrorMessageKey 按 ApiClientError 的 status/reason 选择本地翻译键，注释明确服务端 message 仅作开发诊断；这是前端按自身界面语言渲染错误的实现。

/**
 * Maps a failed request to the only copy the interface may show. The server's `message` stays a developer
 * diagnostic: its stable `reason` and HTTP status choose a translated sentence instead.
 */
export function equipmentErrorMessageKey(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof ApiClientError) {
    if (error.status === 401) return 'equipment.errors.sessionExpired';
    if (error.reason === 'EQUIPMENT_CODE_TAKEN') {
      return 'equipment.errors.codeTaken';
    }
    if (error.status === 404) return 'equipment.errors.notFound';
    if (error.status === 400) return 'equipment.errors.invalidInput';
  }
  return 'equipment.errors.generic';
}

原文件 SHA-256：e42633511fcea1368747f8f94f2fa04eb8bc8cc16c7f29b298667463a9f1b311

E42 · packages/@nocobase/app-testing/README.md:5–9

README 首屏入口表列出 @nocobase/app-testing/server 及其提供的 createAppTest 等夹具，说明公开入口是包说明符而非 dist 目录路径。

| Entry                          | What it gives a test                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `@nocobase/app-testing/server` | `createTestApp()`, the Vitest fixture `createAppTest()`, `createTestAppConfig()`, and `@nocobase/db-testing` |
| `@nocobase/app-testing/cli`    | `bindTestAppCommand()`, `createTestAppConfig()`, and `@nocobase/app-cli/testing`                             |
| `@nocobase/app-testing/client` | `renderWithApp()`, which renders a page inside a started client application                                  |

To check a route's authentication and permission boundaries as a user meets them, start the application itself on test databases with `@nocobase/app-testing/server` and sign in through its own sign-in route with `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`:

import {
  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,
  signIn,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';

业务场景与交付说明

完整双语界面

导航、表头、表单、按钮、状态、空列表和提示均随语言切换，无内部翻译代码。

真实重复编号拦截

两个独立账号并发登记同一编号，服务端返回 409，分别显示英文和中文重复提示，未生成重复记录。

语言与数据持久化

切换英文后刷新仍为英文，用户录入的设备名称和备注保持原文；两台示例设备数据完整。

两个普通测试账号分别保持英文和中文，切换其中一个并刷新另一个，语言互不影响。
